<script type="text/javascript">
    $(document).ready(function(){
         //$('#vednorsourcedropdown').hide();
        // Check Radio-box
        //$(".rating input:radio").attr("checked", false);

        $('.rating input').click(function () {
            $(".rating span").removeClass('checked');
            $(this).parent().addClass('checked');
        });
        //$(".rating input:radio").attr("checked", false);
    });

    function getSearchDetails() 
    {
        var auth_level            =   "1";
        var rating_id             =   $('#rating_id').val();
        var lead_count_id         =   $('#lead_count_id').val();
        var role_id               =   $('#role_id').val();
        var status_id             =   $('#status_id').val();
        var tag_id                =   $('#tag_id').val();
        var design_status         =   $('#design_status').val();
        var user_id               =   $('#user_id').val();
        var roleid                =   $('#roleid').val();
        var project_id            =   $('#project_id').val();
        var designer_role_id      =   $('#designer_role_id').val();
        var brand_id              =   $('#brand_id').val();
        var stages_id             =   $('#stages_id').val();
        var project_id            =   project_id.toString().replace('/','.');

        if(auth_level == '6')
        {
            if(user_id !='')
            {
                role_id = '5';              
            }else
            {
                role_id = 0;
            }
        }else
        {
            if(!role_id){var role_id            = 0;}            
            if(role_id != roleid ){var user_id  = 0;}
        }

        if(rating_id == ''){var rating_id              = 0;}
        if(!lead_count_id){var lead_count_id           = 0;}
        if(!status_id){var status_id                   = 0;}
        if(tag_id == ''){var tag_id                    = 0;}        
        if(!user_id || user_id == ''){var user_id      = 0;}      
        if(project_id == ''){var project_id            = 0;}        
        if(brand_id == ''){var brand_id                = 0;}        
        if(stages_id == ''){var stages_id              = 0;}

        window.location.href = 'https://crm.cookscape.com/lead/assignLeads/'+rating_id+'/'+lead_count_id+'/'+role_id+'/'+status_id+'/'+tag_id+'/'+design_status+'/'+user_id+'/'+project_id+'/'+brand_id+'/'+stages_id; 
    }  
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    //Checkbox show content
  function showContent() 
  {
    var role_id = $('#role_id').val();
    if(role_id==4)
    {
        $(contentDiv).css('display', 'block');
    }else
    {
        $(contentDiv).css('display', 'none');
    }
  }
</script>

        <script type="text/javascript">

function submitDetailsForm() {
            // When the form is submitted
      $("#ajaxModelForm").submit(function()
      { 
        // 'this' refers to the current submitted form 
        var str       = $(this).serialize();
        var actionUrl = $(this).data('action');
        var model_no1  = $(this).data('model_no');
        (typeof model_no1 == 'undefined') ? (model_no='1') : (model_no = model_no1);
        console.log(model_no);
        $.ajax({
        type: "POST",
        url: actionUrl, 
        data: {postdata: str},
        dataType:"html",
          success: function(html1)
          {
            try 
            {
              var parsedJson = JSON.parse(html1);
              if(parsedJson.result == 'success')
              {
                var parsedJson = JSON.parse(html1);
                $('.modal').modal('hide');
                if(parsedJson.URL==null)
                {
                  location.reload();
                }
                else
                {
                  window.location.href = 'https://crm.cookscape.com/'+parsedJson.URL;
                }
              }       
            } 
            catch(e) 
            {
              $(".modal-body").html(html1); // msg in modal body
              $(".modal").modal("show"); // show modal instead alert box
              $('.summernote').summernote({
                height: 100,
                disableResizeEditor: true
              });
            }
          },
        });
      }); // end submit event s
    }

  $(document).ready(function ()
  {       
      // Select2
      $('.select2').select2();

      $('.select2').each(function() {
        $(this).select2({ dropdownParent: $(this).parent()});
    })

      var date = new Date();
      $('.datetimepicker').datetimepicker({
        startDate: date,
        autoclose: true
      });      
  
      $('.single-daterange').daterangepicker(
      {          
        singleDatePicker: true,          
        //autoUpdateInput: false,
        locale: {
                  format: 'YYYY-MM-DD'
                }        
      }); 
      /*function(chosen_date) 
      {          
        $('.single-daterange').val(chosen_date.format('YYYY-MM-DD'));
      });*/

      snRepair = function (node) {
        var w = $('#' + node).width();
        var h = $('#' + node).height();
        var l = (w - $('#' + node).find('.modal-dialog:first').outerWidth())/-2 + 'px';
        $('.note-image-dialog,.note-link-dialog,.note-help-dialog').css('overflow','visible');
        $('.note-image-dialog,.note-link-dialog,.note-help-dialog').css('height',h);
        $('.note-image-dialog,.note-link-dialog,.note-help-dialog').css('width',w);
        $('.note-image-dialog,.note-link-dialog,.note-help-dialog').css('top','-30px');
        $('.note-image-dialog,.note-link-dialog,.note-help-dialog').css('left',l);
      };

      $(window).resize(function() 
      {
        snRepair(name_of_summenrnote_instance);
      })

      $(".closeModal").click(function () 
      {
        $(".modalWindow").modal("hide");
      });

      var openModalsCount = 0;
      $('.modal').on('shown.bs.modal', function() {
          openModalsCount++;
      }).on('hidden.bs.modal', function() {
          openModalsCount--;
          if (openModalsCount > 0) $('body').addClass('modal-open');
      });

      // Toggle Menu
      $('legend').click(function() {
          var $this = $(this);
          var parent = $this.parent();
          var contents = parent.contents().not(this);
          if (contents.length > 0) {
              $this.data("contents", contents.remove());
          } else {
              $this.data("contents").appendTo(parent);
          }
          return false;
      });

      // When the form is submitted
      $("#ajaxModelForm").submit(function()
      { 
        // 'this' refers to the current submitted form 
        var str       = $(this).serialize();
        var actionUrl = $(this).data('action');
        var model_no1  = $(this).data('model_no');
        (typeof model_no1 == 'undefined') ? (model_no='1') : (model_no = model_no1);
        console.log(model_no);
        $.ajax({
        type: "POST",
        url: actionUrl, 
        data: {postdata: str},
        dataType:"html",
          success: function(html1)
          {
            try 
            {
              var parsedJson = JSON.parse(html1);
              if(parsedJson.result == 'success')
              {
                var parsedJson = JSON.parse(html1);
                $('.modal').modal('hide');
                if(parsedJson.URL==null)
                {
                  location.reload();
                }
                else
                {
                  window.location.href = 'https://crm.cookscape.com/'+parsedJson.URL;
                }
              }       
            } 
            catch(e) 
            {
              $(".modal-body").html(html1); // msg in modal body
              $(".modal").modal("show"); // show modal instead alert box
              $('.summernote').summernote({
                height: 100,
                disableResizeEditor: true
              });
            }
          },
        });
      }); // end submit event s


      $("#ajaxModelFormWithFile").submit(function()
      { 
        // 'this' refers to the current submitted form 
        //var str       = $(this).serialize();
        var actionUrl = $(this).data('action');
        $.ajax({
        type: "POST",
        url: actionUrl, 
        data: new FormData( this ),
        processData: false,
        contentType: false, 
          success: function(html1)
          {
            try 
            {
              var parsedJson = JSON.parse(html1);
              if(parsedJson.result == 'success')
              {
                var parsedJson = JSON.parse(html1);
                $('.modal').modal('hide');
                if(parsedJson.URL==null)
                {
                  location.reload();
                }
                else
                {
                  window.location.href = 'https://crm.cookscape.com/'+parsedJson.URL;
                }
              }       
            } 
            catch(e) 
            {
              $(".modal-body").html(html1); // msg in modal body
              $(".modal").modal("show"); // show modal instead alert box
              $('.summernote').summernote({
                height: 100,
                disableResizeEditor: true
              });
            }
          },
        });
      }); // end submit event      

      $("#ajaxLeadDetail").submit(function()
      {
        // 'this' refers to the current submitted form 
        //var str       = $(this).serialize();
        var actionUrl = $(this).data('action');

        setTimeout(function(){
              $('#loaderImg').hide();
        },5000);

         setTimeout(function(){
             $(".modal").modal("hide");
        },6000);

        

        $.ajax({
        type: "POST",
        url: actionUrl, 
        data: new FormData( this ),
        processData: false,
        contentType: false, 
        cache:true,
          success: function(html1)
          {
            try 
            {
              var parsedJson = JSON.parse(html1);
              if(parsedJson.result == 'success')
              {               
                $('.modal').modal('hide');
                if(parsedJson.web_view == 1 && parsedJson.lead_id!=0)
                {
                  location.reload();                     
                }else if(parsedJson.lead_id == '')
                {
                  location.reload();                     
                }else 
                {
                  $.ajax({
                      type : "POST",
                      url  : 'https://crm.cookscape.com/Lead/getLeadDetails/'+parsedJson.ajaxremainder,
                      data : {'lead_id' : parsedJson.lead_id},
                      success : function(data)
                      {
                        $('#LeadDetails').html(data);
                      },
                    });
                }                
              }
            } 
            catch(e) 
            {
              $(".modal-body").html(html1); // msg in modal body
              $(".modal").modal("show"); // show modal instead alert box
              $('.summernote').summernote({
                height: 100,
                disableResizeEditor: true
              });
            }
          },
        });
      }); // end submit event     

      //Alert message fade
      $("#alert-message").fadeTo(2000, 500).slideUp(500, function(){
      $("#alert-message").slideUp(500);
      $("#alert-message").remove();
      });
    
      // Check all and un-check all for HR/attendance
      $("#checkAll").click(function(){
          $(".checkbox").prop("checked", true);
      });

      $("#uncheckAll").click(function(){
          $(".checkbox").prop("checked", false);
      });  
  });

     $.ajax({

  //Add new popup
  function addNewPop(addFormUrl, pkey)
  {
    $.ajax({
    type: "GET",
    url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/"+addFormUrl,
    data: {'pkey_id' : pkey},
    dataType:"html",
        success: function(html1)
        {      
            if(html1 != 'success')
            {                // assigning modal title from parameter
              $(".modal-body").html(html1); // msg in modal body
              $(".modal").modal("show"); // show modal instead alert box
              $('.summernote').summernote({
                height: 100,
                disableResizeEditor: true
              });
            }
          },
      });
  }   

  //Delete function with sweet alert
  function confirmDelete(url)
  {
    if(arguments[0] != null)
    {
      swal({
          title: "Are you sure?",
          text: "You want to delete this???",
          type: "warning",
          showCancelButton: true,
          confirmButtonColor: '#DD6B55',
          confirmButtonText: "Yes",
          cancelButtonText:  "Cancel",
          closeOnConfirm: false,
          closeOnCancel: false
       },
       function(isConfirm)
       {
         if (isConfirm)
         {
            //swal("", "", "success");
            location.href = url;

          } else 
          {
            swal("This operation has been cancelled", "", "error");
            //e.preventDefault();
          }
       });
     
    }
    else
    {
      return false;
    }
    return;
  }

  function confirmComplete(url)
  {

          cancelButtonText:  "Cancel",

       },
       function(isConfirm)
       {
         if (isConfirm)
         {
            //swal("", "", "success");
            location.href = url;

          } else 
          {
            swal("This operation has been cancelled", "", "error");
            //e.preventDefault();
          }
       });
     
    }
    else
    {
      return false;
    }
    return;
  }

  function deactivateUser(task_id, user_id)
  {    
    if(arguments[0] != null)
    {
      swal({
          title: "Are you sure?",
          text: "You want to deactivate the user???",
          type: "warning",
          showCancelButton: true,
          confirmButtonColor: '#DD6B55',
          confirmButtonText: "Yes",
          cancelButtonText:  "Cancel",
          closeOnConfirm: false,
          closeOnCancel: false
       },
       function(isConfirm)
       {
          if (isConfirm)
          {
            $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/Task_list/deactivateUser",
            data: {'task_id' : task_id, 'user_id' : user_id},
            success: function(data)
            {
              location.reload();                
            },
            });

          } else 
          {
            swal("This operation has been cancelled", "", "error");
            //e.preventDefault();
          }
       });
     
    }
    else
    {
      return false;
    }
    return;
  }

  function loadAssignTo()
  {
    var self_assign   = $('#self_assign').is(":checked");
    if(self_assign)
    {
      $('#assignTo').hide();
      $('#appoinmentassignTo').hide();
    }
    else
    {
      $('#assignTo').show();
      $('#appoinmentassignTo').show();
    }
  }

  function loadfeAssignTo()
  {
    var self_assign   = $('#fe_self_assign').is(":checked");
    if(self_assign)
    {
      $('#assignfeTo').hide();
    }
    else
    {
      $('#assignfeTo').show();
    }
  }

  $(document).ready(function(){

    loadAssignTo();
    loadfeAssignTo();
  });

  function removeRow(id) 
  {
    $("#"+id).remove();
  }

  function removeRowimage(id,image_id,path,prodid,url)
  {
    if(arguments[0] != null)
    {
      swal({
          title: "Are you sure?",
          text: "You want to delete???",
          type: "warning",
          showCancelButton: true,
          confirmButtonColor: '#DD6B55',
          confirmButtonText: "Yes",
          cancelButtonText:  "Cancel",
          closeOnConfirm: false,
          closeOnCancel: false
       },
       function(isConfirm)
       {
         if (isConfirm)
         {
              $.ajax({
              type: "GET",
              url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/"+url, 
              data: { 'id'     : id,
                      'img_id' :image_id,
                      'image'  :path
                    },
              dataType:"html",
              success: function(html)
              {
                $("#"+prodid).remove();
                swal("Deleted successfully", "", "success");

              },
            });

          } else 
          {
            swal("This operation has been cancelled", "", "error");
            //e.preventDefault();
          }
       });
     
    }
    else
    {
      return false;
    }
    return;
  }

  function blockleadSource(direct)
  {
    if(direct == 3)
    {
      $('#directLead').hide();
      $('#givenByDropdown').hide();      
      $("#vednor_source").val('').trigger('change');
      $("#lead_source").val('').trigger('change');
    }
    else if(direct == 2)
    {
      $('#directLead').show();
      $("#vednor_source").val('').trigger('change');
      $('#Leadsourcedropdown').show();
      $('#givenByDropdown').show();      
      $('#vednorsourcedropdown').hide();
    }else if(direct == 1)
    {
      $('#directLead').show();
      $("#lead_source").val('').trigger('change');
      $('#Leadsourcedropdown').hide();
      $('#givenByDropdown').hide();      
      $('#vednorsourcedropdown').show();
    }
    else
    {
      $('#directLead').hide();
    }
  }

  function blockpostponedate(direct)
  {
    if(direct == '2')
    {
      $('#Postpone').show();
      $('#showRoom').show();      
    }
    else
    {
      $('#Postpone').hide();      
    }
  }

  function isNumberKey(evt) 
  {
    var charCode = (evt.which) ? evt.which : event.keyCode;
    if ((charCode < 48 || charCode > 57))
        return false;
    return true;
  }

  $(function(){
    $('.myForm').submit(function() {
      $('#loaderImg').show();
      $('.send-mail-head').show();
      
      $('.myForm').hide();
      return true;
    });
  });  

  function addAssignLeads()
  {
    var user_id           = $('#assign_user_id').val();
    var contactable_date  = $('#contactable_date').val();
    var lead_rating       = $('#lead_rating').val();
    var lead_status_id    = $('#lead_status_id').val();

    var sendval = [];
    $('.showcheckBoxes').each(function()
    {
      if ($(this).is(':checked'))
      {
        sendval.push($(this).val());
      }
    }

    );

    if(sendval || (user_id && contactable_date && lead_rating && lead_status_id))
    {
      $.ajax({
      type: "POST",
      url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/Lead_hub/assignMultipleLeads/",
      data: {'user_id' : user_id, 'arrayval' : sendval, 'contactable_date' : contactable_date, 'lead_rating' : lead_rating, 'lead_status_id' : lead_status_id},
      success: function(html1)
      {
        location.reload();          
      },
      });
    }else
    {
      swal({
              title:'',
              text: 'Please Select any inputs ( Assign Users/Contactable Date/Rating/Response Status ) and also select Leads to be edited'
          });
    }
  }

  function deleteDisqualifiedLeads()
  {
    var sendval = [];
    $('.showcheckBoxes').each(function()
    {
      if ($(this).is(':checked'))
      {
        sendval.push($(this).val());
      }
    });

    if(sendval)
    {
      $.ajax({
      type: "POST",
      url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/Lead_hub/deleteDisqualifiedLeads/",
      data: {'arrayval' : sendval},
      success: function(html1)
      {
        location.reload();          
      },
      });
    }else
    {
      swal({
              title:'',
              text: 'Please Select Assign Users or Contactable date and Leads'
          });
    }
  }

   //Add new popup
  function addFeedBack(addFormUrl, pkey, is_accepted, isAdmin, lead_id='', obj)
  {
    if(is_accepted == 1 || isAdmin == 1)
    {
      $.ajax({
      type: "GET",
      url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/"+addFormUrl,
      data: {'pkey_id' : pkey},
      dataType:"html",
          success: function(html1)
          {      
              if(html1 != 'success')
              { // assigning modal title from parameter
                $(".modal-body").html(html1); // msg in modal body
                $(".modal").modal("show"); // show modal instead alert box
              }
            },
        });
    }
    else
    {
       swal({
          title: "Are you sure?",
          text: "You want to accept the lead???",
          type: "warning",
          showCancelButton: true,
          confirmButtonColor: '#DD6B55',
          confirmButtonText: "Yes, I Accept",
          cancelButtonText:  "Cancel",
          closeOnConfirm: true,
          closeOnCancel: false
       },
       function(isConfirm)
       {
         if (isConfirm)
         {
            //$(this).attr("title", 'accepted');
            //$(obj).parent().addClass('active');
            acceptLead(lead_id);
            window.location.reload();
          } else
          {
            swal("This operation has been cancelled", "", "error");
            //e.preventDefault();
          }
       });

      /* swal({
              title:'',
              text: 'Please Accept Leads'
          });
      */
    }
  } 

  function addComments(val)
  {
    tt_description   = $('#tt_description').val();
        
    if(tt_description)
    {
      $.ajax
      ({
        type : "POST",
        dataType:'html',
        url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/Task_list/CreateCommends",
        data : {'tt_description' : tt_description, 't_id' : val},
        success : function(html1)
        {
          $('#addCommendContent').append(html1);
          $('#tt_description').val('');
        }
      });      
    }else
    {
      swal({
              title:'',
              text: 'Please Enter Comments'
          });
    }
  }

  });             

  function loadShape()
  {
    var requirment_shape  = $('#requirment_shape').val();
    if(requirment_shape == '1')
    {
      $('#showWalla').show();
      $('#showWallb').hide();
      $('#showWallc').hide();
    }
    else if(requirment_shape == '2' || requirment_shape == '3')
    {
      $('#showWalla').show();
      $('#showWallb').show();
      $('#showWallc').hide();
    }
    else if(requirment_shape == '4')
    {
      $('#showWalla').show();
      $('#showWallb').show();
      $('#showWallc').show();
    }
    else
    {
      $('#showWalla').hide();
      $('#showWallb').hide();
      $('#showWallc').hide();

    }
  }

  loadShape();

  $(function(){
    var pkey_id = '';
    $(".pushme").click(function () {
      if($('.pushme').text() == 'Start Work')
      {
        val   = 'Start Work';
      }
      else
      {
        val       = 'End Work';
        pkey_id   = $('#pushme').val();
      }
      $.ajax
      ({
        type : "POST",
        dataType:'html',
        url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/Lead/Attendance",
        data : {
                'work_type' : val,
                'pkey_id'   : pkey_id
               },
        success : function(data)
        {
         parseData = JSON.parse(data);
         $('#pushme').val(parseData.edit_id);
        }
      });
      $(this).text(function(i, text){
          return text === "Start Work" ? "End Work" : "Start Work"; 
      });
   });
  });

  $('.pushme').click(function(){
    var $this = $(this);
    $this.toggleClass('pushme');
    if($this.hasClass('pushme')){
        $this.text('Start Work');         
    } else {
        $this.text('End Work');
    }
});

function acceptLead(val)
{
 if(val)
 {
  $.ajax
  ({
    type : "POST",
    dataType:'html',
    url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/Lead/showLeadButtons",
    data : {
            'lead_id' : val
           },
    success : function(html1)
    {
      $('#showLeadButtons').show();
      $('#is_accepted').hide();
      $('#activityLog').html(html1);
    }
  });
  
 }
} 

$( document ).ready(function() 
{
  $('.all_row_values').each(function(i,o)
  {
      bal_amount                 = $(o).find('.bal_amount').val();
      if(bal_amount == '0.00')
      {
          $(o).find('.split_up_id').addClass('select-none');
          $(o).find('.bank_details_id').addClass('select-none');
          $(o).find('.send_email').addClass('select-none');
          $(o).find('.is_advance').addClass('select-none');
          $(o).find('.next_rem_date').addClass('select-none');
          $(o).find('.bank_details_id').attr('readonly', true);
          $(o).find('.split_up_id').attr('readonly', true);
          $(o).find('.percentage').attr('readonly', true);
          $(o).find('.amount').attr('readonly', true);
          $(o).find('.next_rem_date').attr('readonly', true);
          $(o).find('#deletebutton').hide();  
      }

      if($(o).find('.is_advance').prop('checked') == true)
      {
          $('.is_advance').addClass('select-none');
      }
  }
  );

  loadTotalAmount();
});

function loadTotalAmount()
{
  var result = 0;
  $('.amount').each(function()
  {
      result  += +($(this).val());
      $('#total').val(result);
      $('.total').attr('readonly', true);
  }
  );
}

function addNewImage()
{
  row         = $('#imagerow').val();
  j           = Number(row)-1;
  split_up_id = $('#split_up_id'+j).val();

  $.ajax({
  type: "GET",
  url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/order/getSplitupContent", 
  data: { i:row},
  dataType:"html",
  success: function(html)
  {
      loadSplitDrop(j);
      i = Number(row)+1;
      $('#ImageData').append(html);
      $('#imagerow').val(i);
      $('.select2').select2();
      var date = new Date();
      $('.datetimepicker').datetimepicker({
      startDate: date,
      autoclose: true,
  });
  },
});
    
}

function addrequestpage()
{
  row         = $('#imagerow').val();
  j           = Number(row)-1;
  split_up_id = $('#request_id'+j).val();
  lead_id = $('#lead_id').val();

  $.ajax({
  type: "GET",
  url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/Request/getrequestcontent", 
  data: { i:row,
          lead_id : lead_id},
  dataType:"html",
  success: function(html)
  {
      loadscopedropdown(j);
      i = Number(row)+1;
      $('#requestData').append(html);
      $('#imagerow').val(i);
      $('.select2').select2();
      var date = new Date();
      $('.datetimepicker').datetimepicker({
      startDate: date,
      autoclose: true,
  });
  },
});
    
}

{
  row         = $('#imagerow').val();
  j           = Number(row)-1;
  accessories_id = $('#accessories_id'+j).val();

  $.ajax({
  type: "GET",
  url: "https://crm.cookscape.com/order/getAccessoriesContent", 
  data: { i:row},
  dataType:"html",
  success: function(html)
  {
      loadSplitDrop(j);
      i = Number(row)+1;
      $('#ImageData').append(html);
      $('#imagerow').val(i);
      $('.select2').select2();
      var date = new Date();
      $('.datetimepicker').datetimepicker({
      startDate: date,
      autoclose: true,
  });
  },
});
    
}

function isAdvance()
{
  $('.is_advance').addClass('select-none');
}

function loadSplitDrop(j)
{
  k = Number(j) + 1;
  $.ajax({
      type : "POST",
      url  : "https://crm.cookscape.com/Order/getSplitUp",
      data:$("#ajaxLeadDetail").serialize(),
  success : function(data)
  {
    $('#split_up_id'+k).html(data);
  },
  });
}

function loadscopedropdown(j)
{
  k = Number(j) + 1;
  $.ajax({
      type : "POST",
      url  : "https://crm.cookscape.com/Request/getscopedrop",
      data:$("#ajaxLeadDetail").serialize(),
  success : function(data)
  {
    $('#scope_work_id'+k).html(data);
  },
  });
}
</script>

</html>
